In 2023, we navigated through a challenging environment             2023年，經濟和市場逆風撲面而來，影響了我們的
characterized by economic and market headwinds that                 財務表現，我們逆風前行，成功應對各種挑戰。經
affected our financial performance. After several successive        過連續幾年的大幅增長和盈利之後，我們的收入減
years of significant growth and profitability, our revenue          少了30.9%，年內出現虧損。我們的財務表現下降
decreased by 30.9% and incurred a loss for the year. The            的主要原因是，宏觀經濟的不確定性及主要地區的
decline in our financial performance was primarily attributable     不利天氣導致我們的主要客戶採取更為保守的庫存
to more conservative inventory policies adopted by our key          政策。
customers caused by macroeconomic uncertainties and
unfavorable weather in key geographic regions.

deviation from a sustainable growth trajectory we have              貫堅持的可持續增長軌道。具體而言，憑藉我們有
consistently maintained. In particular, with our favorable market   利的市場定位、各銷售點的強勁品牌表現、強大的

robust manufacturing capability and extensive distribution          期逆轉及客戶需求的預期反彈，我們相信於2024年

we believe there will be a corresponding recovery and growth
in our financial performance in 2024 and years to come.

 Business Overview                                             業務概覽

 During the year ended December 31, 2023 (the “Reporting       於 2 0 2 3 年 1 2 月 3 1 日 止 年 度（「 報 告 期 」）， 我 們 經
 Period”), we navigated through a challenging environment      歷了充滿挑戰的經濟和市場逆境。我們的收入由
characterized by economic and market headwinds. Our            2022年的1,989.3百萬美元減少30.9%至2023年的
revenue decreased by 30.9% from US$1,989.3 million in          1,374.7百萬美元，主要是由於宏觀經濟不確定性及
2022 to US$1,374.7 million in 2023, primarily due to more      主要地區的不利天氣，我們的主要客戶採取更為保
conservative inventory policies adopted by our key customers   守的庫存政策。
caused by macroeconomic uncertainties and unfavorable
weather in key geographic regions.

US$811.4 million in 2023, while revenue from our power tools   萬美元，而電動工具分部的收入由2022 年的754.9

our Environmental, Health and Safety (“EHS”) department         境及氣候相關風險。EHS部門識別關鍵環境因素，
and equipment management department are responsible             制定運作控制要求及程序，並進行相關的監測及檢
for managing environmental and climate-related risks            查。EHS部門亦根據關鍵環境因素、適用法律法規
arising from our business operations. The EHS department        及合規評估的變化，每年審查及更新運作控制程序。
identifies key environmental factors, establishes operational
control requirements and procedures and conducts relevant

Considering the macroeconomic uncertainties, the Company         考慮到宏觀經濟的不確定性，本公司已決定調整其
has decided to adjust the development pace of its CHERVON        泉峰智能製造產業園的開發節奏，以適應相關市場
Smart Production Industrial Park to align with such market       變化。因此，部分餘下2021年所得款項淨額將重新
change. Accordingly, part of the remaining 2021 Net Proceeds     分配至產品研發、增強銷售及分銷渠道、支付計息
will be reallocated to researching and developing products,      銀行借款以及營運資金及其他一般企業用途。
enhancing sales and distribution channels, paying interest-
bearing bank borrowings and working capital and other
general corporate purposes.


The Board is of the view that the reallocated use of remaining   董事會認為，重新分配餘下 2021 年所得款項淨額
2021 Net Proceeds will enable the Company to strengthen its      用途將有助本公司加強其競爭地位、更好地滿足客
competitive position, better address customers’ needs, adapt     戶需求、適應不斷變化的市場狀況及提高資金使用
to changing market conditions and improve the efficiency         效率，從而與本公司的戰略目標及營運需要保持一
of the funds utilization, in alignment with the Company’s        致。因此，董事會認為重新分配餘下2021年所得款
strategic objectives and operational needs. Accordingly, the     項淨額用途符合本公司及其股東的整體最佳利益，
Board considers that the reallocated use of the remaining        且不會對本集團的現有業務及營運造成任何重大不
2021 Net Proceeds is in the best interest of the Company and     利影響。
its shareholders as a whole, and will not have any material
adverse impact on the existing business and operations of the
Group.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                      企業管治常規
The Company strives to attain and maintain high standards           本公司相信有效的企業管治架構是提高及保障股東
of corporate governance best suited to the needs and                及其他持份者權益與提升股東價值的基本要素，因
interests of the Group as it believes that an effective corporate   此致力達致及維持最符合本集團需要及利益的高水
governance framework is fundamental to promoting and                平企業管治。因此，本公司已採納及應用企業管治
safeguarding interests of Shareholders and other stakeholders       原則及常規，強調具備一個優秀的董事會、有效的
and enhancing Shareholder value. Accordingly, the Company           風險管理及內部控制系統、嚴格的披露常規、透明
has adopted and applied corporate governance principles             度及問責性，以及與股東及其他持份者進行有效的
and practices that emphasize a quality board of Directors,          溝通及互動。此外，本集團致力不斷提升該等標準
effective risk management and internal control systems,             及常規，並建立穩健的合規及道德管治文化，以配
stringent disclosure practices, transparency and accountability     合本集團的業務營運及常規。
as well as effective communication and engagement with
Shareholders and other stakeholders. It is, in addition,
committed to continuously enhancing these standards and
practices and inculcating a robust culture of compliance and
ethical governance underlying the business operations and
practices across the Group.


The Company has adopted the Corporate Governance Code               本公司已採納上市規則附錄C1所載的企業管治守則

 Provision C.2.1 of the CG Code requires that the roles of            企業管治守則第C.2.1條要求主席與行政總裁的角色
 chairman and chief executive officer be separate and not be          應有區分，並不應由一人同時兼任。
 performed by the same individual.


 However, the Company does not have separate chairman                 然而，本公司並無區分主席及行政總裁，而主席及
 and chief executive officer roles, and the responsibilities of       行政總裁的職責均由潘龍泉先生承擔。董事會相
 both chairman and chief executive officer vest in Mr. Pan            信，由同一人兼任主席及行政總裁的職責，可確保
 Longquan. The Board believes that vesting the responsibilities       本集團內部領導貫徹一致，使本集團的整體策略規
 of both chairman and chief executive officer in the same             劃更有效及更具效率。此外，董事會合共七名董事
 person has the benefit of ensuring the consistent leadership         中有三名獨立非執行董事，董事會內將有足夠的獨
 within the Group and enables more effective and efficient            立意見，以保障本公司及股東的整體利益。
 overall strategic planning of the Group. Besides, with three
 independent non-executive Directors out of a total of seven
 Directors on the Board, there will be sufficient independent
 voice within the Board to protect the interests of the Company
 and the Shareholders as a whole.

Therefore, the Board considers that the balance of power and        因此，董事會認為，現行安排不會損害權力與權限
authority for the present arrangement will not be impaired and      之間的平衡，而此架構將使本公司能夠迅速及有效
this structure will enable the Company to make and implement        地作出及實施決策。董事會將繼續進行檢討，並會
decisions promptly and effectively. The Board will continue         在計及本集團整體情況後考慮於適當時候將董事會

(b)   Basis of preparation of the financial statements              (b)    財務報表編製基準

      The consolidated financial statements of the Group                   本集團截至2023 年12 月31 日止年度的綜合
      for the year ended December 31, 2023 comprise the                    財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司以及本集
      Company and its subsidiaries and the Group’s interest                團於聯營公司的權益。
      in an associate.


      The measurement basis used in the preparation of the                 編製綜合財務報表時使用的計量基準為歷史
      consolidated financial statements is the historical cost             成本法，但以下資產及負債按下文會計政策
      basis except that the following assets and liabilities are           中解釋的公允價值列示。
      stated at their fair value as explained in the accounting
      policies as set out below.


      The preparation of financial statements in conformity                編製符合香港財務報告準則之財務報表要求
      with HKFRSs requires management to make                              管理層須作出判斷、估計及假設，而該等判
      judgements, estimates and assumptions that affect                    斷、估計及假設會影響政策應用以及所呈報
      the application of policies and reported amounts of                  之資產、負債、收入及開支金額。此等估計
      assets, liabilities, income and expenses. The estimates              及相關假設乃基於過往經驗及在有關情況下
      and associated assumptions are based on historical                   被認為屬合理之各項其他因素，而所得結果
      experience and various other factors that are believed               乃用作判斷顯然無法輕易透過其他來源獲得
      to be reasonable under the circumstances, the results                有關資產與負債賬面值之依據。實際結果或
      of which form the basis of making the judgements                     有別於此等估計。
      about carrying values of assets and liabilities that are
      not readily apparent from other sources. Actual results
      may differ from these estimates.


      The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed                此等估計及相關假設會持續審核。若會計估
      on an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates               計的修訂僅影響作出修訂的期間，將會於該
      are recognized in the period in which the estimate is                期間確認，若修訂影響當期及未來期間，則
      revised if the revision affects only that period, or in the          會於作出修訂及其後期間確認。
      period of the revision and future periods if the revision
      affects both current and future periods.


      Judgements made by management in the application                     附註 3 討論了管理層在應用香港財務報告準
      of HKFRSs that have significant effect on the financial              則時作出的對財務報表有重大影響的判斷以
      statements and major sources of estimation uncertainty               及估計不確定性的主要來源。
      are discussed in Note 3.

(d)   Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                   (d)    附屬公司及非控股權益

      Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The              附屬公司乃由本集團所控制的實體。當本集
      Group controls an entity when it is exposed to, or has              團能透過其參與承擔或享有實體可變回報的
      rights to, variable returns from its involvement with the           風險或權利，並能夠向實體使用其權力影響
      entity and has the ability to affect those returns through          回報金額，即對該實體擁有控制權。附屬公
      its power over the entity. The financial statements of              司的財務報表自控制開始日直至控制終止日
      subsidiaries are included in the consolidated financial             併入綜合財務報表。
      statements from the date on which control commences
      until the date on which control ceases.


      Intra-group balances and transactions, and any                      集團內部往來的結餘及交易，以及集團內部
      unrealised income and expenses (except for foreign                  往來的交易所產生的任何未變現收入及開支
      currency transaction gains or losses) arising from intra-          （外幣交易收益或虧損除外）均予以抵銷。集
      group transactions, are eliminated. Unrealised losses               團內部交易所產生的未變現虧損的抵銷方法
      resulting from intra-group transactions are eliminated              與未變現收益相同，但只限於沒有減值證據
      in the same way as unrealised gains, but only to the                的情況下。
      extent that there is no evidence of impairment.


      Changes in the Group’s interests in a subsidiary that               本集團將不導致喪失控股權的附屬公司權益
      do not result in a loss of control are accounted for as             的變動按權益交易方式入賬。
      equity transactions.


      When the Group loses control of a subsidiary, it                    當本集團失去對附屬公司的控制權時，其終
      derecognizes the assets and liabilities of the subsidiary,          止確認附屬公司的資產及負債以及任何相關
      and any related non-controlling interests (“NCI”) and               非控股權益（「非控股權益」）及其他權益部
      other components of equity. Any resulting gain or loss              分。任何所產生的盈虧於損益中確認。任何
      is recognized in profit or loss. Any interest retained in           在喪失控股權日仍保留於該前度附屬公司的
      that former subsidiary is measured at fair value when               權益按公允價值確認。
      control is lost.

          An associate is an entity in which the Group or the                 聯營公司為本集團或本公司對其有重大影響
          Company has significant influence, but not control or               力但對其財務及經營政策並無控制權或聯合

          equity method, unless it is classified as held for sale (or         類為持作出售（或列入分類為持作出售的出

          profit or loss and other comprehensive income (“OCI”)               止。
          of those investees, until the date on which significant
          influence ceases.

      Credit-impaired financial assets                                    信貸減值金融資產


      At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether a                於各報告日期，本集團評估金融資產有否出
      financial asset is credit-impaired. A financial asset is            現信貸減值。當發生一項或多項對金融資產
      credit-impaired when one or more events that have a                 估計未來現金流量產生不利影響的事件時，
      detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows               金融資產即被視為出現信貸減值。
      of the financial asset have occurred.


      Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired                  金融資產出現信貸減值的證據包括以下可觀
      includes the following observable events:                           察事件：


      –      significant financial difficulties of the debtor;            －         債務人面對重大財務困難；


      –      a breach of contract, such as a default or                   －         違反合約，如利息或本金付款違約或
             delinquency in interest or principal payments;                         拖欠；


      –      the restructuring of a loan or advance by the                －         本集團根據其他情況下不會考慮之條
             Group on terms that the Group would not                                款重組貸款或墊款；
             consider otherwise;


      –      it is probable that the debtor will enter into               －         債務人有可能破產或需要進行其他財
             bankruptcy or other financial reorganization; or                       務重組；或


      –      the disappearance of an active market for a                  －         發行人陷入財困導致證券失去活躍市
             security because of financial difficulties of the                      場。
             issuer.


      Write-off policy                                                    撇銷政策


      The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written           若日後回收不可實現時，金融資產的賬面總
      off to the extent that there is no realistic prospect of            值將撇銷。該情況通常出現在本集團以其他
      recovery. This is generally the case when the Group                 方式確定債務人並無資產或收入來源可產生
      otherwise determines that the debtor does not have                  足夠現金流量以償還撇銷金額。
      assets or sources of income that could generate
      sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts subject to
      the write-off.


      Subsequent recoveries of an asset that was previously               過往撇銷資產的後續回收於回收期間於損益
      written off are recognized as a reversal of impairment in           確認為減值撥回。
      profit or loss in the period in which the recovery occurs.

 (ii)     Impairment of other non-current assets                         (ii)   其他非流動資產減值

          At each reporting date, the Group reviews the                         於各報告日期，本集團審閱其非金融資產
          carrying amounts of its non-financial assets (other                   （存貨及遞延稅項資產除外）的賬面值，以釐
          than inventories and deferred tax assets) to determine                定是否有任何減值跡象。倘存在任何有關跡
          whether there is any indication of impairment. If any                 象，則會估計資產的可收回金額。每年對商
          such indication exists, then the asset’s recoverable                  譽進行減值測試。
          amount is estimated. Goodwill is tested annually for
          impairment.


          For impairment testing, assets are grouped together                   就減值測試而言，資產納入持續使用產生現
          into the smallest group of assets that generates cash                 金流入的最小組別，該組別基本獨立於其他
          inflows from continuing use that are largely independent              資產或現金產生單位（「現金產生單位」）的現
          of the cash inflows of other assets or cash-generating                金流入。業務合併產生的商譽分配至預期受
          units (“CGU”s). Goodwill arising from a business                      益於合併協同效應的現金產生單位或現金產
          combination is allocated to CGUs or groups of CGUs                    生單位組別。
          that are expected to benefit from the synergies of the
          combination.


          The recoverable amount of an asset or CGU is the                      資產或現金產生單位的可收回金額為其使用
          greater of its value in use and its fair value less costs of          價值與其公允價值減出售成本兩者中的較高
          disposal. Value in use is based on the estimated future               者。使用價值乃基於估計未來現金流量，後
          cash flows, discounted to their present value using                   者使用能反映現時市場對金錢的時間價值的
          a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market                  評估及該資產或現金產生單位的特有風險的
          assessments of the time value of money and the risks                  除稅前折現率折減至其現值。
          specific to the asset or CGU.


          An impairment loss is recognized if the carrying amount               倘資產或現金產生單位的賬面值超過其可收
          of an asset or CGU exceeds its recoverable amount.                    回金額，則確認減值虧損。


          Impairment losses are recognized in profit or loss. They              減值虧損於損益內確認。減值虧損首先用以
          are allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any              調低分攤至現金產生單位的任何商譽的賬面
          goodwill allocated to the CGU, and then to reduce the                 值，然後按比例調低現金產生單位內其他資
          carrying amounts of the other assets in the CGU on a                  產的賬面值。
          pro rata basis.


          An impairment loss in respect of goodwill is not                      有關商譽之減值虧損將不予撥回。就其他資
          reversed. For other assets, an impairment loss is                     產而言，僅當所產生的賬面值不超過假設並
          reversed only to the extent that the resulting carrying               無確認減值虧損而應釐定的賬面值（已扣除
          amount does not exceed the carrying amount that                       折舊或攤銷）方可撥回減值虧損。
          would have been determined, net of depreciation
          or amortization, if no impairment loss had been
          recognized.

        normal operating capacity.

      differences is insufficient to recognize a deferred                      司的業務計劃，考慮未來應課稅溢利，並就
      tax asset in full, then future taxable profits, adjusted                 現有暫時差額的撥回作出調整。遞延稅項資
      for reversals of existing temporary differences, are                     產於各報告日期審閱，並在相關稅務利益不
      considered, based on the business plans for individual                   再可能實現的情況下予以扣減；當未來應課
      subsidiaries in the Group. Deferred tax assets are                       稅利潤的可能性提高時，此類減少將被逆
      reviewed at each reporting date and are reduced to the                   轉。
      extent that it is no longer probable that the related tax
      benefit will be realized; such reductions are reversed
      when the probability of future taxable profits improves.

 (u)      Provisions and contingent liabilities                         (u)   撥備及或有負債

          Generally provisions are determined by discounting the              一般而言，撥備乃按反映當時市場對貨幣時
          expected future cash flows at a pre-tax rate that reflects          間價值及負債特定風險的評估的除稅前利率
          current market assessment of the time value of money                貼現預期未來現金流量而釐定。
          and the risks specific to the liability.


          A provision for warranties is recognized when the                   保修撥備乃於相關產品或服務售出時根據過
          underlying products or services are sold, based on                  往保修數據及權衡保修可能出現之結果及有
          historical warranty data and a weighting of possible                關之可能性確認。
          outcomes against their associated probabilities.


          A provision for onerous contracts is measured at the                多項虧損性合約之撥備乃按終止合約預期成
          present value of the lower of the expected cost of                  本及繼續合約成本淨額（以較低者為準）的現
          terminating the contract and the expected net cost of               值計量，而繼續合約的預期凈成本乃根據履
          continuing with the contract, which is determined based             行該合約項下責任的增量成本及與履行該合
          on the incremental costs of fulfilling the obligation under         約直接相關的其他成本的分配釐定。於計提
          that contract and an allocation of other costs directly             撥備前，本集團就該合約相關資產確認任何
          related to fulfilling that contract. Before a provision is          減值虧損（見附註2(k)(ii)）。
          established, the Group recognizes any impairment loss
          on the assets associated with that contract (see note 2
          (k)(ii)).


          Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                如果經濟利益不大可能流出，或無法對有關
          benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                  數額作出可靠估計，則會將該責任披露為或
          estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                有負債，除非經濟利益流出的可能性極微。
          contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow             其存在僅能以一宗或多宗未來事件的發生與
          of economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations,               否來確定的潛在責任，除非經濟利益流出的
          whose existence will only be confirmed by the                       可能性極微，否則亦披露為或有負債。
          occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future
          events are also disclosed as contingent liabilities
          unless the probability of outflow of economic benefits is
          remote.


          Where some or all of the expenditure required to settle             倘負債計提準備的部分或全部支出預計將由
          a provision is expected to be reimbursed by another                 另一方償還，則針對幾乎確定的預期償還確
          party, a separate asset is recognized for any expected              認一項單獨的資產。確認的報帳金額僅限於
          reimbursement that would be virtually certain. The                  負債計提準備的賬面金額。
          amount recognized for the reimbursement is limited to
          the carrying amount of the provision.

          employees who retire in the form of life-long annuities.         休金福利。此與一般的德國市場做法一致，

 (d)      Nature and purpose of reserves                                (d)   儲備性質及目的

          (i)      Other reserve                                              (i)    其他儲備

                    the Share Incentive Scheme to the directors and                  公允價值部分。
                    employees of the Group.


          (ii)     PRC statutory reserve                                      (ii)   中國法定儲備

                    Statutory reserve is established in accordance                   法定儲備按照相關中國規則和規例以
                    with the relevant PRC rules and regulations and                  及在中國註冊成立的本集團旗下各公
                    the articles of association of the companies                     司的組織章程細則計提。
                    comprising the Group which are incorporated in
                    the PRC.


                    In accordance with the PRC Company Law,                          根據中國公司法，屬國內企業的本集
                    certain subsidiaries of the Group which are                      團旗下若干附屬公司須分配其除稅後
                    domestic enterprises are required to allocate                    利潤的10%（按相關中國會計準則釐
                    10% of their profit after tax, as determined in                  定）至其各自的法定儲備，直至儲備
                    accordance with the relevant PRC accounting                      達到其各自註冊資本的50%。對於有
                    standards, to their respective statutory reserves                關實體而言，法定儲備可用於彌補過
                    until the reserves reach 50% of their respective                 往年度的虧損（如有），並且可按投資
                    registered capital. For the entity concerned,                    者現有股本權益的比例轉為資本，惟
                    statutory reserves can be used to make good                      儲備結餘在轉換後不少於該實體註冊
                    previous years’ losses, if any, and may be                       資本的25%。
                    converted into capital in proportion to the
                    existing equity interests of investors, provided
                    that the balance of the reserve after such
                    conversion is not less than 25% of the entity’s
                    registered capital.

      (iii)   Exchange reserve                                           (iii)     匯兌儲備

              exchange differences arising from the translation                    的經營財務報表引起的所有外匯差
              of the financial statements of operations with                       額。該儲備乃根據附註2(x)所載的會
              functional currency other than USD. The reserve                      計政策處理。
              is dealt with in accordance with the accounting
              policy as set out in Note 2(x).


(e)   Capital management                                          (e)    資本管理

      The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital               本集團管理資本的首要目標乃保障本集團能
      are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a              夠持續經營，以便透過對與風險水平相應的
      going concern, so that it can continue to provide returns          產品及服務定價以及獲得合理成本的融資，
      for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,              繼續為股東創造回報及為其他持份者帶來利
      by pricing products and services commensurately with               益。
      the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a
      reasonable cost.


      The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages               本集團定期積極對資本架構進行檢討及管
      its capital structure to maintaining a balance between             理，以在較高股東回報情況下可能伴隨的較
      the higher shareholders returns that might be possible             高借貸水平與良好資本狀況帶來的好處及保
      with higher levels of borrowings and the advantages                障之間取得平衡，並根據經濟狀況的變化對
      and security afforded by a sound capital position, and             資本架構作出調整。
      makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
      changes in economic conditions.


      The Group monitors its capital structure on the basis of           本集團利用淨資本負債比率監察其資本架
      a net gearing ratio. For this purpose, the Group defines           構。就此而言，本集團將淨負債界定為流動
      net debt as total current and non-current bank loans               及非流動銀行貸款及租賃負債總額減現金及
      and lease liabilities less cash and cash equivalents               現金等價物及已抵押存款。本集團將資本界
      and pledged deposits. The Group defines capital as                 定為包括所有權益組成部分。
      including all components of equity.

33    FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND FAIR                            33     財務風險管理及金融工具的公允價值
      VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS
      Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency            本集團業務面臨正常業務過程中產生的信
      risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s                     貸、流動性、利率及貨幣風險。
      business.


      The Group’s exposure to these risks and financial risk               本集團面臨的該等風險及本集團為管理該等
      management policies and practices used by the Group                  風險而採用的財務風險管理政策及慣例描述
      to manage these risks are described below:                           如下：


(a)   Credit risk                                                   (a)    信貸風險

      Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will              信貸風險指對手方將違反合約義務而導致本
      default on its contractual obligations resulting in a                集團產生財務虧損的風險。本集團信貸風險
      financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is              主要歸因於貿易及其他應收款項。本集團面
      primarily attributable to trade and other receivables.               對來自現金及現金等價物、已抵押存款及應
      The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash                收票據的信貸風險有限，原因是對手方為信
      and cash equivalents, pledged deposits and bills                     譽狀況良好的知名金融機構，本集團認為其
      receivable is limited because the counterparties are                 信貸風險較低。
      reputable financial institutions with high credit standing,
      for which the Group considers to have low credit risk.


      Except for the financial guarantees given by the Group               除附註35所載由本集團提供的財務擔保外，
      as set out in Note 35 the Group does not provide any                 本集團並無任何其他將令本集團承受信貸風
      other guarantees which would expose the Group to                     險的擔保。於報告期末有關該等財務擔保的
      credit risk. The maximum exposure to credit risk in                  最高信貸風險敞口乃披露於附註35。
      respect of these financial guarantees at the end of the
      reporting period is disclosed in Note 35.


      Trade receivables                                                    貿易應收款項

      The Group has established a credit risk management                   本集團已制定信貸風險管理政策，據此對所
      policy under which individual credit evaluations are                 有需要超過一定金額的信貸的客戶均須進行
      performed on all customers requiring credit over                     個別信貸評估。該等評估集中於客戶過往到
      a certain amount. These evaluations focus on the                     期作出付款的記錄及目前的支付能力，並計
      customer’s past history of making payments when                      及客戶的特定資料以及有關客戶經營所在經
      due and current ability to pay and take into account                 濟環境的資料。貿易應收款項自賬單日期起
      information specific to the customer as well as                      計30 至180 日內到期。正常情況下，本集團
      pertaining to the economic environment in which the                  不會收取客戶的抵押品。
      customer operates. Trade receivables are due within
      30 to 180 days from the date of billing. Normally, the
      Group does not obtain collateral from customers.

          is calculated using a provision matrix. The Group                      撥備矩陣計算得出。由於不同地區的虧損模
          segments its trade receivables based on geographic                     式不同，本集團根據地區劃分其貿易應收款
          regions, due to different loss patterns experienced in                 項。
          the different regions.

(b)   Liquidity risk                                               (b)    流動性風險

      Individual operating entities within the Group are                  本集團旗下個別經營實體負責各自現金管
      responsible for their own cash management, including                理，包括現金盈餘短期投資及為滿足預期現
      the short-term investment of cash surpluses and the                 金要求而籌集貸款，惟借款超出一定授權預
      raising of loans to cover expected cash demands,                    定水平後須取得母公司董事會批准。本集團
      subject to approval by the parent company’s board                   的政策為定期監察其流動資金需求及其遵守
      when the borrowings exceed certain predetermined                    借貸契諾的情況，以確保維持充足的現金儲
      levels of authority. The Group’s policy is to regularly             備及來自主要金融機構的足夠承諾額度以滿
      monitor its liquidity requirements and its compliance               足其短期及長期的流動資金需求。
      with lending covenants to ensure that it maintains
      sufficient reserves of cash and adequate committed
      lines of funding from major financial institutions to meet
      its liquidity requirements in the short and longer term.

     to improve our long-term incentive mechanism to attract          心僱員的積極性。於2024年2月20日，採納
     and retain outstanding talents and to fully mobilize the         2024年股份計劃的決議案已於股東特別大會

     At December 31, 2023, the Directors of the Company               於2023 年12 月31 日，本公司董事認為，本
     consider the immediate parent of the Group is                    集團的直接母公司為德潤控股有限公司，該
     Panmercy Holdings Limited, a company incorporated                公司於香港註冊成立。該實體並無編製可供
     in Hong Kong. This entity does not produce financial             公眾使用的財務報表。
